# ── Helpers de registry ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def load_registry():
    if not os.path.exists(REGISTRY_PATH):
        save_registry(DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
        return DEFAULT_REGISTRY.copy()

    try:
        with open(REGISTRY_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            reg = json.load(f)

        if not isinstance(reg, dict) or len(reg) == 0:
            save_registry(DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
            return DEFAULT_REGISTRY.copy()

        return reg
    except Exception:
        save_registry(DEFAULT_REGISTRY)
        return DEFAULT_REGISTRY.copy()


def save_registry(registry):
    with open(REGISTRY_PATH, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(registry, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)


def registry_choices():
    reg = load_registry()
    return list(reg.keys())


def refresh_model_dropdown():
    choices = registry_choices()
    value = choices[0] if choices else None
    return gr.update(choices=choices, value=value)


# ── Torch dtype helper ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def get_torch_dtype(name):
    if name == "float16":
        return torch.float16
    if name == "bfloat16":
        return torch.bfloat16
    if name == "float32":
        return torch.float32
    return torch.float16


# ── Helpers Hugging Face ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
api = HfApi()


def hf_login(token):
    token = (token or "").strip()
    if not token:
        return "Nenhum token informado."

    try:
        login(token=token, add_to_git_credential=False)
        return "✅ Login no Hugging Face realizado."
    except Exception as e:
        return f"❌ Falha no login HF: {e}"


def repo_exists(repo):
    try:
        api.repo_info(repo)
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


def list_repo_files_safe(repo):
    try:
        return list(api.list_repo_files(repo))
    except Exception:
        return []


def pick_repo_for_t4(cfg):
    candidates = []

    if cfg.get("t4_repo"):
        candidates.append(cfg["t4_repo"])

    candidates.extend(cfg.get("t4_repo_candidates", []))

    if cfg.get("repo"):
        candidates.append(cfg["repo"])

    seen = []
    for c in candidates:
        if c and c not in seen:
            seen.append(c)
            if repo_exists(c):
                return c

    return cfg.get("repo")


def detect_backend(repo):
    if not repo:
        return "unknown"

    if repo == "DeepBeepMeep/krea-2":
        return "wan2gp_krea2"

    files = list_repo_files_safe(repo)

    if not files:
        return "unknown"

    if "model_index.json" in files:
        return "diffusers"

    if any("Krea2Turbo" in f for f in files):
        return "wan2gp_krea2"

    if any(f.lower().endswith((".safetensors", ".ckpt", ".pt")) for f in files):
        return "single_file"

    return "unknown"


# ── Download helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def download_krea(cfg):
    REPO = cfg.get("repo", "DeepBeepMeep/krea-2")
    QWEN_IMG_REPO = "DeepBeepMeep/Qwen_image"

    def dl(repo, filename, dest_dir, dest_name=None):
        Path(dest_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        name = dest_name or os.path.basename(filename)
        fp = os.path.join(dest_dir, name)

        if os.path.exists(fp):
            print(f"  ✓ {name} already downloaded")
            return

        print(f"  ↓ Downloading {name} ...")
        hf_hub_download(
            repo_id=repo,
            filename=filename,
            local_dir=dest_dir
        )

        tmp = os.path.join(dest_dir, filename)
        if os.path.exists(tmp) and os.path.abspath(tmp) != os.path.abspath(fp):
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(fp), exist_ok=True)
            shutil.move(tmp, fp)

        print(f"  ✅ {name}")

    # Transformer
    dl(
        REPO,
        "Krea2Turbo_quanto_bf16_int8.safetensors",
        MODELS_DIR
    )

    # Text encoder
    TE = "Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct"
    te_files = [
        "Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct_quanto_bf16_int8.safetensors",
        "config.json",
        "tokenizer.json",
        "tokenizer_config.json",
        "chat_template.jinja",
        "preprocessor_config.json"
    ]

    for f in te_files:
        dl(
            REPO,
            f"{TE}/{f}",
            os.path.join(MODELS_DIR, TE),
            dest_name=f
        )

    # VAE
    for f in ["qwen_vae.safetensors", "qwen_vae_config.json"]:
        dl(QWEN_IMG_REPO, f, MODELS_DIR)

    # Limpar cache
    cache_dir = os.path.join(MODELS_DIR, ".cache")
    if os.path.exists(cache_dir):
        try:
            shutil.rmtree(cache_dir)
        except Exception:
            pass


def download_diffusers(cfg):
    selected_repo = cfg.get("selected_repo") or cfg.get("repo")
    if not selected_repo:
        raise ValueError("Repo não definido para modelo Diffusers.")

    folder_name = cfg.get("folder_name", "diffusers-model")
    local_dir = os.path.join(MODELS_DIR, folder_name)
    cfg["model_path"] = local_dir

    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(local_dir, "model_index.json")):
        print(f"  ✓ Modelo Diffusers já baixado em {local_dir}")
        return

    print(f"  ↓ Baixando {selected_repo} para {local_dir} ...")

    snapshot_download(
        repo_id=selected_repo,
        local_dir=local_dir,
        ignore_patterns=cfg.get("ignore_patterns", COMMON_IGNORE)
    )

    if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(local_dir, "model_index.json")):
        print("  ⚠️ model_index.json não encontrado. Talvez o repo não seja Diffusers padrão.")


def download_single_file(cfg):
    selected_repo = cfg.get("selected_repo") or cfg.get("repo")
    if not selected_repo:
        raise ValueError("Repo não definido para modelo single-file.")

    folder_name = cfg.get("folder_name", "single-file-model")
    local_dir = os.path.join(MODELS_DIR, folder_name)
    Path(local_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    files = list_repo_files_safe(selected_repo)
    candidates = [
        f for f in files
        if f.lower().endswith((".safetensors", ".ckpt", ".pt"))
    ]

    if not candidates:
        raise ValueError("Nenhum arquivo .safetensors/.ckpt/.pt encontrado no repositório.")

    remote_file = candidates[0]
    local_path = os.path.join(local_dir, os.path.basename(remote_file))

    if os.path.exists(local_path):
        cfg["model_path"] = local_path
        print(f"  ✓ {os.path.basename(remote_file)} já existe.")
        return

    print(f"  ↓ Baixando {remote_file} ...")
    hf_hub_download(
        repo_id=selected_repo,
        filename=remote_file,
        local_dir=local_dir
    )

    cfg["model_path"] = local_path
    print(f"  ✅ {os.path.basename(remote_file)}")


# ── CIVITAI DOWNLOADER ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def parse_civitai_url(url_or_id):
    """Extrai o ID do modelo da URL do Civitai ou retorna o ID diretamente."""
    if url_or_id.isdigit():
        return url_or_id
    
    # Tenta extrair de https://civitai.com/models/12345/nome-do-modelo
    match = re.search(r'civitai\.com/models/(\d+)', url_or_id)
    if match:
        return match.group(1)
    
    # Tenta extrair de https://civitai.com/api/v1/models/12345
    match = re.search(r'api/v1/models/(\d+)', url_or_id)
    if match:
        return match.group(1)
        
    return None

def download_from_civitai(url_or_id, token):
    """Baixa o modelo do Civitai e retorna o caminho local e metadados."""
    model_id = parse_civitai_url(url_or_id)
    if not model_id:
        raise ValueError("URL ou ID do Civitai inválido.")

    api_url = f"https://civitai.com/api/v1/models/{model_id}"
    headers = {}
    if token and token.strip():
        headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {token.strip()}"

    print(f"🔎 Consultando API do Civitai para o modelo ID: {model_id}...")
    response = requests.get(api_url, headers=headers)
    
    if response.status_code != 200:
        raise ValueError(f"Falha ao consultar Civitai (Status {response.status_code}). Verifique o ID ou Token.")

    data = response.json()
    model_name = data.get("name", "unknown_model")
    versions = data.get("modelVersions", [])
    
    if not versions:
        raise ValueError("Nenhuma versão encontrada para este modelo.")

    # Pega a versão mais recente (primeira da lista)
    latest_version = versions[0]
    version_id = latest_version.get("id")
    base_model = latest_version.get("baseModel", "unknown") # Ex: "SDXL 1.0", "Flux.1 D", "SD 1.5"
    version_name = latest_version.get("name", "v1")
    
    files = latest_version.get("files", [])
    if not files:
        raise ValueError("Nenhum arquivo encontrado nesta versão.")

    # Procura o arquivo principal (geralmente o do tipo "Model" ou o primeiro .safetensors)
    target_file = None
    for f in files:
        if f.get("type") == "Model" or f.get("name", "").endswith(".safetensors"):
            target_file = f
            break
            
    if not target_file:
        target_file = files[0] # Fallback para o primeiro arquivo

    download_url = target_file.get("downloadUrl")
    file_name = target_file.get("name", f"model_{version_id}.safetensors")
    
    if not download_url:
        raise ValueError("URL de download não encontrada.")

    # Sanitiza nome do arquivo
    safe_name = re.sub(r'[^\w\-.]', '_', file_name)
    local_path = os.path.join(CIVITAI_DIR, safe_name)

    if os.path.exists(local_path):
        print(f"  ✓ {safe_name} já existe.")
        return local_path, base_model, model_name

    print(f"  ↓ Baixando {safe_name} do Civitai...")
    print(f"     Base Model detectado: {base_model}")
    
    with requests.get(download_url, headers=headers, stream=True) as r:
        r.raise_for_status()
        total_size = int(r.headers.get('content-length', 0))
        with open(local_path, 'wb') as f:
            for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=8192):
                f.write(chunk)

    print(f"  ✅ {safe_name} baixado com sucesso.")
    return local_path, base_model, model_name


def ensure_downloaded(cfg):
    backend = cfg.get("backend", "auto")

    selected_repo = pick_repo_for_t4(cfg)
    cfg["selected_repo"] = selected_repo

    if backend in [None, "", "auto"]:
        backend = detect_backend(selected_repo)
        cfg["backend"] = backend

    if backend == "wan2gp_krea2":
        download_krea(cfg)
    elif backend == "diffusers":
        download_diffusers(cfg)
    elif backend == "single_file":
        download_single_file(cfg)
    else:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Backend desconhecido: {backend}. "
            "Use auto, diffusers, wan2gp_krea2 ou single_file."
        )

    return cfg


# ── Unload ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def unload_current_model():
    for key in ["pipe", "krea_model"]:
        if STATE.get(key) is not None:
            try:
                del STATE[key]
            except Exception:
                pass

    STATE["pipe"] = None
    STATE["krea_model"] = None
    STATE["config"] = None
    STATE["backend"] = None
    STATE["loaded"] = False
    STATE["lora_name"] = None
    STATE["lora_scale"] = 0.0

    gc.collect()
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()


# ── Load Diffusers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def load_diffusers(cfg):
    from diffusers import DiffusionPipeline

    model_path = cfg.get("model_path") or cfg.get("selected_repo") or cfg.get("repo")

    if not os.path.isdir(model_path):
        model_path = cfg.get("selected_repo") or cfg.get("repo")
    else:
        # Se a pasta local não tiver model_index.json, tenta cair direto no repo
        if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(model_path, "model_index.json")):
            fallback_repo = cfg.get("selected_repo") or cfg.get("repo")
            if fallback_repo:
                model_path = fallback_repo

    dtype = get_torch_dtype(cfg.get("dtype", "float16"))

    load_kwargs = {
        "torch_dtype": dtype,
        "low_cpu_mem_usage": True,
    }

    if cfg.get("use_safetensors", True):
        load_kwargs["use_safetensors"] = True

    try:
        pipe = DiffusionPipeline.from_pretrained(model_path, **load_kwargs)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Falha ao carregar com use_safetensors=True: {e}")
        load_kwargs.pop("use_safetensors", None)
        pipe = DiffusionPipeline.from_pretrained(model_path, **load_kwargs)

    offload_mode = cfg.get("cpu_offload", "model")

    if offload_mode == "sequential":
        try:
            pipe.enable_sequential_cpu_offload()
            print("✅ Sequential CPU offload ativado.")
        except Exception:
            try:
                pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()
                print("✅ Model CPU offload ativado como fallback.")
            except Exception:
                pipe.to("cuda")
    elif offload_mode == "model":
        try:
            pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()
            print("✅ Model CPU offload ativado.")
        except Exception:
            pipe.to("cuda")
    else:
        try:
            pipe.to("cuda")
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        pipe.enable_vae_tiling()
        print("✅ VAE tiling ativado.")
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        pipe.enable_vae_slicing()
        print("✅ VAE slicing ativado.")
    except Exception:
        pass

    STATE["pipe"] = pipe
    STATE["backend"] = "diffusers"
    STATE["loaded"] = True


# ── Load single file (SD 1.5, SDXL, Pony, Illustrious, FLUX) ─────────────────
def load_single_file(cfg):
    model_path = cfg.get("model_path")
    base_model = cfg.get("base_model_type", "unknown") # Ex: "SDXL 1.0", "Flux.1 D", "SD 1.5"
    
    if not model_path or not os.path.exists(model_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError("Arquivo single-file não encontrado.")

    dtype = get_torch_dtype(cfg.get("dtype", "float16"))
    
    pipe = None
    is_safetensors = model_path.lower().endswith(".safetensors")
    
    # 1. FLUX
    if "flux" in base_model.lower() or "flux" in model_path.lower():
        try:
            from diffusers import FluxPipeline
            print("ℹ️ Tentando carregar como FluxPipeline...")
            pipe = FluxPipeline.from_single_file(
                model_path,
                torch_dtype=dtype,
                use_safetensors=is_safetensors
            )
            print("✅ Carregado como FLUX.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Falha como FLUX: {e}")
            
    # 2. SDXL / Pony / Illustrious
    if pipe is None and ("sdxl" in base_model.lower() or "pony" in base_model.lower() or "illustrious" in base_model.lower() or "sdxl" in model_path.lower()):
        try:
            from diffusers import StableDiffusionXLPipeline
            print("ℹ️ Tentando carregar como StableDiffusionXLPipeline...")
            pipe = StableDiffusionXLPipeline.from_single_file(
                model_path,
                torch_dtype=dtype,
                use_safetensors=is_safetensors
            )
            print("✅ Carregado como SDXL/Pony/Illustrious.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Falha como SDXL: {e}")
            
    # 3. SD 1.5 (Fallback)
    if pipe is None:
        try:
            from diffusers import StableDiffusionPipeline
            print("ℹ️ Tentando carregar como StableDiffusionPipeline (SD 1.5)...")
            pipe = StableDiffusionPipeline.from_single_file(
                model_path,
                torch_dtype=dtype,
                use_safetensors=is_safetensors
            )
            print("✅ Carregado como SD 1.5.")
        except Exception as e:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"❌ Falha ao carregar single-file. O modelo não parece ser FLUX, SDXL nem SD 1.5 compatível.\n"
                f"Erro final: {e}"
            )

    # Configuração de Offload
    offload_mode = cfg.get("cpu_offload", "model")

    if offload_mode == "sequential":
        try: pipe.enable_sequential_cpu_offload()
        except: 
            try: pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()
            except: pipe.to("cuda")
    elif offload_mode == "model":
        try: pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()
        except: pipe.to("cuda")
    else:
        try: pipe.to("cuda")
        except: pass

    STATE["pipe"] = pipe
    STATE["backend"] = "single_file"
    STATE["loaded"] = True


# ── Load Wan2GP Krea ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def load_wan2gp_krea(cfg):
    sys.path.insert(0, WAN2GP_DIR)

    from mmgp import offload
    from shared.utils import files_locator as fl
    from models.krea2.krea2_handler import family_handler

    paths = cfg.get("paths")
    if not paths:
        raise ValueError(
            "Para modelos wan2gp_krea2, o registry precisa conter 'paths' com transformer, text_encoder e vae."
        )

    fl.set_checkpoints_paths([
        MODELS_DIR,
        os.path.join(WAN2GP_DIR, "ckpts"),
        WAN2GP_DIR,
        "."
    ])

    transformer_path = paths["transformer"]
    text_encoder_path = paths["text_encoder"]

    if not os.path.exists(transformer_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Transformer não encontrado em {transformer_path}\n"
            "Baixe o modelo primeiro."
        )

    if not os.path.exists(text_encoder_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Text encoder não encontrado em {text_encoder_path}\n"
            "Baixe o modelo primeiro."
        )

    dtype = get_torch_dtype(cfg.get("dtype", "float16"))
    vae_dtype = get_torch_dtype(cfg.get("vae_dtype", "float16"))

    old_cwd = os.getcwd()
    os.chdir(WAN2GP_DIR)

    try:
        base_model_type = cfg.get("base_model_type", "krea2_turbo")
        model_type = cfg.get("model_type", "krea2_turbo")

        model_def = family_handler.query_model_def(base_model_type, {})

        krea_model, pipe = family_handler.load_model(
            model_filename=transformer_path,
            model_type=model_type,
            base_model_type=base_model_type,
            model_def=model_def,
            quantizeTransformer=cfg.get("quantize_transformer", False),
            dtype=dtype,
            VAE_dtype=vae_dtype,
            text_encoder_filename=text_encoder_path,
        )

        offload_cfg = cfg.get("offload", {})

        offload.profile(
            pipe,
            profile_no=offload_cfg.get("profile_no", 2),
            quantizeTransformer=cfg.get("quantize_transformer", False),
            convertWeightsFloatTo=dtype,
            pinnedMemory=offload_cfg.get("pinnedMemory", False),
            asyncTransfers=offload_cfg.get("asyncTransfers", False),
            budgets=offload_cfg.get(
                "budgets",
                {
                    "transformer": 10000,
                    "text_encoder": 4000,
                    "vae": 1500,
                    "*": 500
                }
            ),
        )

        offload.shared_state["_attention"] = cfg.get("attention", "sdpa")

        STATE["krea_model"] = krea_model
        STATE["pipe"] = pipe
        STATE["backend"] = "wan2gp_krea2"
        STATE["loaded"] = True

    finally:
        os.chdir(old_cwd)


# ── Load dispatcher ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def load_model(cfg):
    unload_current_model()

    backend = cfg.get("backend", "auto")

    if backend == "wan2gp_krea2":
        load_wan2gp_krea(cfg)
    elif backend == "diffusers":
        load_diffusers(cfg)
    elif backend == "single_file":
        load_single_file(cfg)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Backend não suportado: {backend}")

    STATE["config"] = cfg
    gc.collect()
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()


# ── UI settings helper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def model_ui_settings(model_name):
    reg = load_registry()
    cfg = reg.get(model_name, {})

    if not cfg:
        return (
            gr.update(),
            gr.update(),
            gr.update(),
            gr.update(),
            gr.update(),
            ""
        )

    steps = cfg.get("steps", {"min": 1, "max": 30, "default": 8})
    images = cfg.get("images", {"min": 1, "max": 1, "default": 1})

    return (
        gr.update(
            minimum=steps.get("min", 1),
            maximum=steps.get("max", 30),
            value=steps.get("default", 8)
        ),
        gr.update(
            minimum=images.get("min", 1),
            maximum=images.get("max", 1),
            value=images.get("default", 1)
        ),
        gr.update(
            choices=cfg.get("resolutions", ["1024px (Standard)"]),
            value=cfg.get("default_resolution", "1024px (Standard)")
        ),
        gr.update(
            choices=cfg.get("aspect_ratios", ["1:1 Square"]),
            value=cfg.get("default_aspect_ratio", "1:1 Square")
        ),
        gr.update(value=float(cfg.get("guide_scale", 0.0))),
        cfg.get("warning", "")
    )


def initial_ui():
    choices = registry_choices()
    value = choices[0] if choices else None
    return [gr.update(choices=choices, value=value)] + list(model_ui_settings(value))


# ── Manager actions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def detect_selected_model(model_name, token):
    try:
        msg_login = hf_login(token) if token else ""
        reg = load_registry()
        cfg = reg.get(model_name)

        if not cfg:
            return "Modelo não encontrado."

        repo = pick_repo_for_t4(cfg)
        backend = detect_backend(repo)

        msg = (
            f"Modelo: {model_name}\n"
            f"Repo escolhido: {repo}\n"
            f"Backend detectado: {backend}"
        )

        if msg_login:
            msg = msg_login + "\n\n" + msg

        return msg
    except Exception as e:
        return f"❌ Erro ao detectar: {e}"


def download_and_load_model(model_name, token):
    try:
        if token:
            hf_login(token)

        reg = load_registry()
        cfg = reg.get(model_name)

        if not cfg:
            return ["❌ Modelo não encontrado."] + [gr.update()] * 6

        cfg = cfg.copy()
        cfg["name"] = model_name

        cfg["selected_repo"] = pick_repo_for_t4(cfg)

        if cfg.get("backend", "auto") in [None, "", "auto"]:
            cfg["backend"] = detect_backend(cfg["selected_repo"])

        cfg = ensure_downloaded(cfg)
        load_model(cfg)

        status = (
            f"✅ Modelo carregado: {model_name}\n"
            f"Repo usado: {cfg.get('selected_repo')}\n"
            f"Backend: {cfg.get('backend')}"
        )

        return [status] + list(model_ui_settings(model_name))

    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        return [f"❌ Erro: {e}"] + [gr.update()] * 6


# ── CIVITAI LOADER ACTION ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def load_civitai_model(url_or_id, token):
    try:
        if not url_or_id or not url_or_id.strip():
            return "❌ Por favor, insira a URL ou ID do modelo.", gr.update()

        local_path, base_model, model_name = download_from_civitai(url_or_id.strip(), token)
        
        # Cria uma configuração temporária para o modelo baixado
        cfg = {
            "label": f"Civitai: {model_name}",
            "backend": "single_file",
            "model_path": local_path,
            "base_model_type": base_model,
            "dtype": "float16",
            "cpu_offload": "model", # SDXL/Pony geralmente funciona bem com model offload
            "supports_negative": True,
            "supports_guidance": True,
            "guide_scale": 7.0 if "flux" not in base_model.lower() else 0.0,
            "steps": {"min": 1, "max": 30, "default": 20 if "flux" not in base_model.lower() else 4},
            "images": {"min": 1, "max": 1, "default": 1},
            "resolutions": ["512px (Fast)", "768px (Balanced)", "1024px (High)"],
            "default_resolution": "768px (Balanced)" if "sdxl" in base_model.lower() else "512px (Fast)",
            "aspect_ratios": ["1:1 Square", "16:9 Landscape", "9:16 Portrait"],
            "default_aspect_ratio": "1:1 Square",
            "warning": f"Modelo baixado do Civitai. Base: {base_model}"
        }
        
        # Ajusta offload para FLUX (mais pesado)
        if "flux" in base_model.lower():
            cfg["cpu_offload"] = "sequential"
            cfg["default_resolution"] = "768px (Low VRAM)"
            cfg["resolutions"] = ["512px (Fast)", "768px (Low VRAM)", "1024px (Standard)"]
            
        load_model(cfg)
        
        status = (
            f"✅ Modelo Civitai carregado: {model_name}\n"
            f"Base Model: {base_model}\n"
            f"Caminho: {os.path.basename(local_path)}"
        )
        
        # Atualiza a UI com as configurações do modelo
        return status, gr.update(value=f"Civitai: {model_name}")

    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        return f"❌ Erro ao carregar do Civitai: {e}", gr.update()


# ── LoRA ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def apply_lora(lora_source, weight):
    try:
        if not STATE.get("loaded"):
            return "❌ Nenhum modelo carregado. Baixe e carregue um modelo primeiro."

        if STATE.get("backend") not in ["diffusers", "single_file"]:
            return "❌ Nesta versão, LoRA é suportado apenas para modelos Diffusers."

        pipe = STATE.get("pipe")
        if pipe is None:
            return "❌ Pipeline não disponível."

        lora_source = (lora_source or "").strip()

        if not lora_source:
            try:
                pipe.unload_lora_weights()
                STATE["lora_name"] = None
                STATE["lora_scale"] = 0.0
                return "✅ LoRA removido."
            except Exception as e:
                return f"❌ Falha ao remover LoRA: {e}"

        if os.path.exists(lora_source):
            pipe.load_lora_weights(lora_source)
            STATE["lora_name"] = lora_source
        else:
            if "/" in lora_source and lora_source.lower().endswith((".safetensors", ".ckpt", ".pt")):
                repo_id, weight_name = lora_source.split("/", 1)
                pipe.load_lora_weights(repo_id, weight_name=weight_name)
                STATE["lora_name"] = f"{repo_id}:{weight_name}"
            else:
                pipe.load_lora_weights(lora_source)
                STATE["lora_name"] = lora_source

        STATE["lora_scale"] = float(weight)

        try:
            pipe.set_adapters(["default"], adapter_weights=[float(weight)])
        except Exception:
            pass

        return f"✅ LoRA aplicado: {STATE['lora_name']} | peso: {STATE['lora_scale']}"

    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        return f"❌ Erro ao aplicar LoRA: {e}"


# ── Prompt enhancer ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _enhance(prompt, style_preset="None"):
    if not prompt:
        return ""

    p = prompt.lower()

    if any(k in p for k in ["photorealistic", "hyperrealistic", "highly detailed", "8k resolution"]):
        enhanced = prompt

    elif any(w in p for w in ["person", "woman", "man", "girl", "boy", "portrait", "face"]):
        enhanced = prompt + ", 85mm lens, f/1.8, natural skin texture, professional studio lighting, highly detailed, 8k"

    elif any(w in p for w in ["cyberpunk", "futuristic", "sci-fi", "robot", "neon"]):
        enhanced = prompt + ", cyberpunk aesthetic, neon glow, volumetric fog, cinematic lighting, ray tracing, 8k"

    elif any(w in p for w in ["forest", "mountain", "ocean", "landscape", "lake", "river", "sky", "sunset"]):
        enhanced = prompt + ", golden hour, national geographic style, volumetric mist, wide-angle lens, 8k"

    elif any(w in p for w in ["dragon", "magic", "wizard", "elf", "castle", "fantasy"]):
        enhanced = prompt + ", glowing magical particles, ethereal light, highly detailed fantasy art, 8k"

    elif any(w in p for w in ["anime", "illustration", "drawing", "painting", "digital art"]):
        enhanced = prompt + ", vibrant color palette, clean line art, beautiful lighting, anime key visual, masterpiece"

    else:
        enhanced = prompt + ", highly detailed, photorealistic, cinematic lighting, masterpiece, 8k"

    styles = {
        "Cinematic": "cinematic lighting, dramatic shadows, film grain, masterpiece",
        "Photographic": "professional photography, 35mm lens, f/2.8, depth of field, photorealistic",
        "Anime": "anime key visual, vibrant color palette, clean line art, highly detailed",
        "Cyberpunk": "cyberpunk aesthetic, neon lights, volumetric smoke, high contrast",
        "Fantasy": "mythical atmosphere, glowing particles, ethereal light, digital painting",
    }

    if style_preset and style_preset != "None" and style_preset in styles:
        enhanced += ", " + styles[style_preset]

    return enhanced


# ── Resolution helper ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def _parse_px(value, default=1024):
    try:
        if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
            return int(value)
        return int(str(value).split("px")[0].strip())
    except Exception:
        return default


def _dims(aspect, base):
    b = _parse_px(base, 1024)

    if aspect == "1:1 Square":
        return b, b

    elif aspect == "16:9 Landscape":
        h = int((b * 9 / 16) // 16 * 16)
        return b, max(16, h)

    elif aspect == "9:16 Portrait":
        w = int((b * 9 / 16) // 16 * 16)
        return max(16, w), b

    elif aspect == "4:3 Standard":
        h = int((b * 3 / 4) // 16 * 16)
        return b, max(16, h)

    elif aspect == "3:4 Portrait":
        w = int((b * 3 / 4) // 16 * 16)
        return max(16, w), b

    return b, b


# ── Backend generate ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def backend_generate(seed, prompt, negative_prompt, steps, width, height, guidance_scale, callback=None):
    backend = STATE.get("backend")

    if backend == "wan2gp_krea2":
        krea_model = STATE.get("krea_model")
        if krea_model is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Krea model não carregado.")

        result = krea_model.generate(
            seed=int(seed),
            input_prompt=prompt,
            n_prompt=negative_prompt.strip() or None,
            sampling_steps=int(steps),
            width=int(width),
            height=int(height),
            guide_scale=float(guidance_scale),
            batch_size=1,
            callback=callback,
            loras_slists={"phase1": []},
        )

        if result is None:
            raise RuntimeError("A geração retornou None.")

        img = Image.fromarray(result[:, 0].permute(1, 2, 0).numpy())

        del result
        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

        return img

    elif backend in ["diffusers", "single_file"]:
        pipe = STATE.get("pipe")
        if pipe is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Pipeline Diffusers não carregado.")

        generator = torch.Generator(device="cuda").manual_seed(int(seed))

        kwargs = {
            "prompt": prompt,
            "num_inference_steps": int(steps),
            "width": int(width),
            "height": int(height),
            "generator": generator,
        }

        cfg = STATE.get("config") or {}

        if cfg.get("supports_negative", True) and negative_prompt:
            kwargs["negative_prompt"] = negative_prompt

        if cfg.get("supports_guidance", True):
            kwargs["guidance_scale"] = float(guidance_scale)

        if STATE.get("lora_scale", 0.0) not in [None, 0.0]:
            try:
                sig = inspect.signature(pipe.__call__)
                if "cross_attention_kwargs" in sig.parameters:
                    kwargs["cross_attention_kwargs"] = {
                        "scale": float(STATE["lora_scale"])
                    }
            except Exception:
                pass

        try:
            sig = inspect.signature(pipe.__call__)
            if "negative_prompt" not in sig.parameters:
                kwargs.pop("negative_prompt", None)
            if "guidance_scale" not in sig.parameters:
                kwargs.pop("guidance_scale", None)
            if "cross_attention_kwargs" not in sig.parameters:
                kwargs.pop("cross_attention_kwargs", None)
        except Exception:
            pass

        result = None

        if callback is not None:
            try:
                sig = inspect.signature(pipe.__call__)
                if "callback_on_step_end" in sig.parameters:
                    def step_cb(pipe_obj, step_index, timestep, callback_kwargs):
                        callback(step_index, None, False)
                        return callback_kwargs

                    result = pipe(callback_on_step_end=step_cb, **kwargs)
                else:
                    result = pipe(**kwargs)
            except TypeError:
                result = pipe(**kwargs)
        else:
            result = pipe(**kwargs)

        if result is None or not hasattr(result, "images") or len(result.images) == 0:
            raise RuntimeError("A geração Diffusers não retornou imagens.")

        img = result.images[0]

        del result
        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

        return img

    else:
        raise RuntimeError("Nenhum backend carregado.")


# ── Generate function ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
@torch.inference_mode()
def generate(
    prompt,
    negative_prompt,
    steps,
    aspect_ratio,
    resolution,
    seed,
    num_images,
    guidance_scale,
    progress=gr.Progress(track_tqdm=True)
):
    try:
        if not STATE.get("loaded"):
            yield gr.update(value=None, selected_index=None), "❌ Nenhum modelo carregado. Use a aba Modelo.", gr.update(visible=False)
            return

        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

        yield gr.update(value=None, selected_index=None), "⏳ Starting...", gr.update(visible=False)

        width, height = _dims(aspect_ratio, resolution)

        init_seed = random.randint(0, 2**32 - 1) if (seed is None or seed < 0) else int(seed)

        images = []
        seeds_used = []

        t0 = time.time()

        num_images = int(num_images)
        steps = int(steps)

        for i in range(num_images):
            s = (init_seed + i) % (2**32)
            seeds_used.append(s)

            print(f"Generating image {i+1}/{num_images}: {width}x{height}, steps={steps}, seed={s}")
            sys.stdout.flush()

            def cb(step_idx, latent, is_start, override_num_inference_steps=None, **kw):
                pct = (i + (step_idx + 1) / max(1, steps)) / max(1, num_images)
                progress(
                    min(pct, 0.99),
                    desc=f"Image {i+1}/{num_images} — step {step_idx + 1}/{steps}"
                )

            progress((i) / max(1, num_images), desc=f"Preparing image {i+1}/{num_images}...")

            img = backend_generate(
                seed=s,
                prompt=prompt,
                negative_prompt=negative_prompt,
                steps=steps,
                width=width,
                height=height,
                guidance_scale=guidance_scale,
                callback=cb
            )

            images.append(img)

            gc.collect()
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()

            yield gr.update(value=images, selected_index=0), \
                  f"⏳ Generated {len(images)}/{num_images}...", \
                  gr.update(visible=False)

        zip_path = None

        if len(images) > 1:
            import zipfile
            import tempfile

            tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".zip", delete=False)
            zip_path = tmp.name

            model_label = (STATE.get("config") or {}).get("label", "model")
            safe_label = model_label.replace(" ", "_").lower()

            with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w") as zf:
                for idx, img in enumerate(images):
                    p = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=".png")
                    img.save(p)
                    zf.write(p, f"{safe_label}_{idx+1}_seed{seeds_used[idx]}.png")
                    try:
                        os.remove(p)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

        elapsed = time.time() - t0

        status = (
            f"✅ {len(images)} image(s) in {elapsed:.1f}s | "
            f"Seeds: {seeds_used} | {width}x{height}"
        )

        yield gr.update(value=images, selected_index=0), status, \
              gr.update(value=zip_path, visible=(zip_path is not None))

    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()

        yield gr.update(value=None, selected_index=None), f"❌ Error: {e}", gr.update(visible=False)


# ── Admin: adicionar novo modelo ─────────────────────────────────────────────
def save_new_model(
    label,
    repo,
    t4_repo,
    backend,
    dtype,
    cpu_offload,
    steps_min,
    steps_max,
    steps_default,
    guidance_default,
    resolutions,
    default_resolution,
    aspects,
    default_aspect,
    supports_negative,
    supports_guidance
):
    try:
        label = (label or "").strip()
        repo = (repo or "").strip()
        t4_repo = (t4_repo or "").strip()

        if not label or not repo:
            return "❌ Nome e repo são obrigatórios.", gr.update()

        reg = load_registry()

        res_list = [x.strip() for x in (resolutions or "").split(",") if x.strip()]
        if not res_list:
            res_list = ["1024px (Standard)"]

        asp_list = [x.strip() for x in (aspects or "").split(",") if x.strip()]
        if not asp_list:
            asp_list = ["1:1 Square"]

        folder_name = label.lower().replace(" ", "-").replace("/", "-")

        reg[label] = {
            "label": label,
            "repo": repo,
            "t4_repo": t4_repo,
            "t4_repo_candidates": [t4_repo, repo] if t4_repo else [repo],
            "backend": backend or "auto",
            "folder_name": folder_name,
            "dtype": dtype or "float16",
            "cpu_offload": cpu_offload or "model",
            "use_safetensors": True,
            "supports_negative": bool(supports_negative),
            "supports_guidance": bool(supports_guidance),
            "guide_scale": float(guidance_default or 0.0),
            "steps": {
                "min": int(steps_min or 1),
                "max": int(steps_max or 30),
                "default": int(steps_default or 8)
            },
            "images": {
                "min": 1,
                "max": 1,
                "default": 1
            },
            "resolutions": res_list,
            "default_resolution": (default_resolution or res_list[0]).strip(),
            "aspect_ratios": asp_list,
            "default_aspect_ratio": (default_aspect or asp_list[0]).strip(),
            "styles": STYLES,
            "ignore_patterns": COMMON_IGNORE,
            "warning": "Modelo adicionado manualmente pelo painel."
        }

        save_registry(reg)

        choices = list(reg.keys())
        return f"✅ Modelo '{label}' salvo no registry.", gr.update(choices=choices, value=label)

    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        return f"❌ Erro ao salvar modelo: {e}", gr.update()


# ── Clear helper ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
def clear_all():
    cfg = STATE.get("config") or {}
    steps = cfg.get("steps", {"default": 8})
    images = cfg.get("images", {"default": 1})

    return (
        "",
        "",
        steps.get("default", 8),
        cfg.get("default_aspect_ratio", "1:1 Square"),
        cfg.get("default_resolution", "1024px (Standard)"),
        -1,
        images.get("default", 1),
        "None",
        float(cfg.get("guide_scale", 0.0)),
        gr.update(value=None, selected_index=None),
        "",
        gr.update(visible=False)
    )


# ── Gradio UI ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CIVITAI_EXAMPLES = [
    "Arthemy Comics Anima (2700278)",
    "Arthemy Comics Illustrious (1273254)",
    "Arthemy Western Art (2241572)",
    "Arthemy Comics Krea2 (2759057)",
    "Arthemy Toons Illustrious (1906150)",

    "Kestral Flux Anime (697877)",

    "Arthemy Comics Anima (2700278)": "https://civitai.com/models/2700278",
    "Arthemy Comics Illustrious (1273254)": "https://civitai.com/models/1273254",
    "Arthemy Western Art (2241572)": "https://civitai.com/models/2241572",
    "Arthemy Comics Krea2 (2759057)": "https://civitai.com/models/2759057",

def update_civitai_url(choice):
    if choice in CIVITAI_URL_MAP:
        return CIVITAI_URL_MAP[choice]
    return ""


with gr.Blocks(theme=gr.themes.Soft(), title="Advanced Multi-Model Image Studio") as demo:
    gr.Markdown("# 🎨 Advanced Multi-Model Image Studio")
    gr.Markdown(
        "Escolha, baixe e carregue modelos diretamente na interface. "
        "Suporte a **Civitai**, Hugging Face, Krea-2-Turbo, FLUX, SDXL e muito mais."
    )

    with gr.Tabs():
        with gr.TabItem("🧠 Modelo / Configuração"):
            hf_token = gr.Textbox(
                label="🔑 Hugging Face Token (opcional)",
                type="password",
                placeholder="Cole aqui se o modelo exigir login/licença"
            )

            model_dropdown = gr.Dropdown(
                label="📦 Modelo (Hugging Face / Wan2GP)",
                choices=registry_choices(),
                value=registry_choices()[0] if registry_choices() else None
            )

            model_warning = gr.Markdown("")

            with gr.Row():
                detect_btn = gr.Button("🔎 Detectar backend", variant="secondary")
                load_btn = gr.Button("⬇️ Baixar e carregar (HF)", variant="primary")
                refresh_btn = gr.Button("🔄 Atualizar lista", variant="secondary")

            manager_status = gr.Textbox(label="ℹ️ Status", lines=6)

            gr.Markdown("---")
            gr.Markdown("### 🎨 Civitai Downloader")
            gr.Markdown("Baixe e carregue modelos diretamente do Civitai. O app detecta automaticamente se é SD 1.5, SDXL, Pony, Illustrious ou FLUX.")
            
            with gr.Row():
                civitai_examples = gr.Dropdown(
                    label="Exemplos Populares",
                    choices=[""] + CIVITAI_EXAMPLES,
                    value=""
                )
                
            civitai_url = gr.Textbox(
                label="URL ou ID do Modelo no Civitai",
                placeholder="https://civitai.com/models/12345 ou apenas 12345"
            )
            
            civitai_token = gr.Textbox(
                label="🔑 Civitai API Token (Recomendado)",
                type="password",
                placeholder="Cole seu token do Civitai aqui (Configurações > API Keys)"
            )
            
            civitai_load_btn = gr.Button("⬇️ Baixar do Civitai e Carregar", variant="primary")
            civitai_status = gr.Textbox(label="Status Civitai", lines=4)

            with gr.Accordion("🧩 LoRA (após carregar modelo Diffusers)", open=False):
                lora_source = gr.Textbox(
                    label="LoRA (caminho local ou repo HF)",
                    placeholder="Ex: /content/lora.safetensors ou usuario/lora-repo/lora.safetensors"
                )
                lora_weight = gr.Slider(0.0, 2.0, 1.0, step=0.05, label="LoRA Weight")
                apply_lora_btn = gr.Button("🧩 Aplicar LoRA", variant="primary")
                lora_status = gr.Textbox(label="LoRA Status", lines=2)

            with gr.Accordion("➕ Adicionar novo modelo sem mexer no código", open=False):
                admin_label = gr.Textbox(label="Nome do modelo", placeholder="Ex: Meu SDXL Custom")
                admin_repo = gr.Textbox(label="Repo HF", placeholder="usuario/modelo")
                admin_t4_repo = gr.Textbox(label="Repo T4/quantizado opcional", placeholder="usuario/modelo-int8")

                admin_backend = gr.Dropdown(
                    ["auto", "diffusers", "wan2gp_krea2", "single_file"],
                    value="auto",
                    label="Backend"
                )

                admin_dtype = gr.Dropdown(
                    ["float16", "bfloat16", "float32"],
                    value="float16",
                    label="Dtype"
                )

                admin_offload = gr.Dropdown(
                    ["model", "sequential", "none"],
                    value="model",
                    label="CPU Offload"
                )

                with gr.Row():
                    admin_steps_min = gr.Number(1, label="Steps mín")
                    admin_steps_max = gr.Number(30, label="Steps máx")
                    admin_steps_default = gr.Number(8, label="Steps padrão")
                    admin_guidance = gr.Number(0.0, label="Guidance padrão")

                admin_resolutions = gr.Textbox(
                    label="Resoluções (separadas por vírgula)",
                    value="512px (Fast),768px (Balanced),1024px (High)"
                )

                admin_default_resolution = gr.Textbox(
                    label="Resolução padrão",
                    value="768px (Balanced)"
                )

                admin_aspects = gr.Textbox(
                    label="Aspect ratios (separados por vírgula)",
                    value="1:1 Square,16:9 Landscape,9:16 Portrait"
                )

                admin_default_aspect = gr.Textbox(
                    label="Aspect ratio padrão",
                    value="1:1 Square"
                )

                admin_negative = gr.Checkbox(True, label="Suporta Negative Prompt")
                admin_guidance_support = gr.Checkbox(True, label="Suporta Guidance Scale")

                admin_save_btn = gr.Button("💾 Salvar novo modelo", variant="primary")
                admin_status = gr.Textbox(label="Admin Status", lines=2)

        with gr.TabItem("🎨 Gerar"):
            with gr.Row():
                with gr.Column(scale=3):
                    prompt = gr.Textbox(
                        label="📝 Prompt",
                        lines=3,
                        placeholder="A small dark cat walking down an abandoned cobblestone street at dusk..."
                    )

                    with gr.Row():
                        enhance_btn = gr.Button("✨ Enhance Prompt", variant="secondary")
                        style_preset = gr.Dropdown(STYLES, value="None", label="🎨 Style Preset")

                    with gr.Row():
                        aspect_ratio = gr.Dropdown(
                            ["1:1 Square", "16:9 Landscape", "9:16 Portrait"],
                            value="1:1 Square",
                            label="📐 Aspect Ratio"
                        )

                        resolution = gr.Dropdown(
                            ["512px (Fast)", "768px (Balanced)", "1024px (Standard)"],
                            value="768px (Balanced)",
                            label="🖥️ Resolution"
                        )

                    with gr.Row():
                        steps = gr.Slider(1, 30, 8, step=1, label="⚡ Steps")
                        num_images = gr.Slider(1, 1, 1, step=1, label="🖼️ Images")

                    with gr.Accordion("⚙️ Advanced", open=False):
                        negative_prompt = gr.Textbox(
                            label="🚫 Negative Prompt",
                            placeholder="low quality, blurry, distorted, bad anatomy",
                            lines=2
                        )

                        seed = gr.Number(-1, label="🎲 Seed (-1 = random)", precision=0)
                        guidance_scale = gr.Slider(0.0, 20.0, 0.0, step=0.5, label="🎚️ Guidance Scale")

                    with gr.Row():
                        gen_btn = gr.Button("🎨 Generate", variant="primary", size="lg")
                        clear_btn = gr.Button("🗑️ Clear", variant="secondary", size="lg")

                with gr.Column(scale=1):
                    gallery = gr.Gallery(
                        label="🖼️ Output",
                        columns=2,
                        rows=2,
                        object_fit="contain",
                        preview=True
                    )

                    zip_out = gr.File(label="📦 Download ZIP", visible=False)
                    status_out = gr.Textbox(label="ℹ️ Status", interactive=False)

    # Eventos
    demo.load(
        fn=initial_ui,
        outputs=[model_dropdown, steps, num_images, resolution, aspect_ratio, guidance_scale, model_warning]
    )

    model_dropdown.change(
        fn=model_ui_settings,
        inputs=[model_dropdown],
        outputs=[steps, num_images, resolution, aspect_ratio, guidance_scale, model_warning]
    )

    refresh_btn.click(
        fn=refresh_model_dropdown,
        outputs=[model_dropdown]
    )

    detect_btn.click(
        fn=detect_selected_model,
        inputs=[model_dropdown, hf_token],
        outputs=[manager_status]
    )

    load_btn.click(
        fn=download_and_load_model,
        inputs=[model_dropdown, hf_token],
        outputs=[manager_status, steps, num_images, resolution, aspect_ratio, guidance_scale, model_warning]
    )

    civitai_examples.change(
        fn=update_civitai_url,
        inputs=[civitai_examples],
        outputs=[civitai_url]
    )

    civitai_load_btn.click(
        fn=load_civitai_model,
        inputs=[civitai_url, civitai_token],
        outputs=[civitai_status, model_dropdown]
    )

    apply_lora_btn.click(
        fn=apply_lora,
        inputs=[lora_source, lora_weight],
        outputs=[lora_status]
    )

    admin_save_btn.click(
        fn=save_new_model,
        inputs=[
            admin_label,
            admin_repo,
            admin_t4_repo,
            admin_backend,
            admin_dtype,
            admin_offload,
            admin_steps_min,
            admin_steps_max,
            admin_steps_default,
            admin_guidance,
            admin_resolutions,
            admin_default_resolution,
            admin_aspects,
            admin_default_aspect,
            admin_negative,
            admin_guidance_support
        ],
        outputs=[admin_status, model_dropdown]
    )

    enhance_btn.click(
        fn=lambda p, s: _enhance(p, s),
        inputs=[prompt, style_preset],
        outputs=[prompt]
    )

    gen_btn.click(
        fn=generate,
        inputs=[
            prompt,
            negative_prompt,
            steps,
            aspect_ratio,
            resolution,
            seed,
            num_images,
            guidance_scale
        ],
        outputs=[gallery, status_out, zip_out]
    )

    clear_btn.click(
        fn=clear_all,
        outputs=[
            prompt,
            negative_prompt,
            steps,
            aspect_ratio,
            resolution,
            seed,
            num_images,
            style_preset,
            guidance_scale,
            gallery,
            status_out,
            zip_out
        ]
    )

print("Launching Advanced Multi-Model Image Studio...")

demo.queue()
demo.launch(share=True, inline=False, debug=True, show_error=True)

import ast

path = "/content/advanced_app.py"

try:

    ast.parse(source)

    print("\nO aplicativo está pronto. Execute a Cell 4 para iniciar!")

# Como usar o novo recurso do Civitai

1.  **Execute as células 1 a 4** na ordem.
2.  Abra o link público do Gradio gerado na Cell 4.
3.  Na aba **🧠 Modelo / Configuração**, role para baixo até a seção **🎨 Civitai Downloader**.
4.  **Token da API:** É altamente recomendável colar seu token do Civitai (vá em *Configurações > API Keys* no site do Civitai e gere um token). Sem ele, modelos que exigem login podem falhar.
5.  **Escolha o modelo:** Você pode selecionar um dos exemplos populares no dropdown (como *Arthemy Comics Anima* ou *Kestral Flux Anime*) ou colar qualquer URL/ID do Civitai no campo de texto.
6.  Clique em **⬇️ Baixar do Civitai e Carregar**.
7.  O app vai:
    *   Consultar a API do Civitai.
    *   Baixar o arquivo `.safetensors` mais recente.
    *   Detectar automaticamente se é **SD 1.5, SDXL, Pony, Illustrious ou FLUX**.
    *   Carregar o pipeline correto do Diffusers.
8.  Vá para a aba **🎨 Gerar** e crie suas imagens!

O sistema agora suporta nativamente todos os modelos que você listou e qualquer outro checkpoint disponível no Civitai.

### ASSISTANT
Esse erro (`ImportError: cannot import name '_center' from 'numpy._core.umath'`) é um problema clássico do Google Colab. 

### O que aconteceu?
Durante a instalação das dependências na Cell 1, algum pacote forçou a atualização do **NumPy para a versão 2.x**. Como o Colab já havia carregado o NumPy 1.x na memória do kernel antes de você rodar a célula, o Python fica "confuso": ele tenta usar as extensões C antigas que estão na RAM, mas lê os arquivos novos da versão 2.x que estão no disco. Isso quebra a importação de qualquer biblioteca científica (como `diffusers` e `torch`).

### Como resolver (Passo Obrigatório)
Você **precisa reiniciar o ambiente de execução** para limpar a memória do NumPy antigo.

1. No menu superior do Colab, clique em **Ambiente de execução (Runtime)** > **Reiniciar ambiente de execução (Restart runtime)**. (Ou use o atalho `Ctrl+M .`).
2. Após reiniciar, **execute a Cell 1 novamente**.
3. Em seguida, execute a **nova Cell 1.5** abaixo, que já força o downgrade do NumPy para a versão estável (`1.26.4`) e corrige as outras dependências.

---

### 🔧 Nova Cell 1.5 (Com correção de NumPy)

```python
# 🔧 Cell 1.5 — Fix NumPy & Dependencies
import os

print("=== Fixing NumPy and dependencies ===")
# Remove qualquer resíduo do numpy 2.x e instala a versão 1.26.4 (padrão estável do Colab)
!pip uninstall -y numpy
!pip install -q --timeout 120 "numpy==1.26.4"

# Corrige huggingface_hub para versão compatível com transformers
!pip install -q --timeout 120 "huggingface_hub==0.34.4"
!pip install -q --timeout 120 "transformers==4.54.0" "tokenizers==0.21.4"
!pip install -q --timeout 120 "protobuf>=4.25,<6"

# Garante diffusers atualizado para suportar os modelos do Civitai
!pip install -q --timeout 120 --upgrade diffusers

print("\n=== Validating versions ===")
try:
    import numpy as np
    print(f"numpy          : {np.__version__}")
    import transformers
    import huggingface_hub
    import diffusers
    print(f"transformers   : {transformers.__version__}")
    print(f"huggingface_hub: {huggingface_hub.__version__}")
    print(f"diffusers      : {diffusers.__version__}")
    
    import torch
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        props = torch.cuda.get_device_properties(0)
        print(f"\nGPU  : {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
        print(f"VRAM : {props.total_memory / 1024**3:.1f} GB")
        
    print("\n✅ Tudo certo! Pode ir para a Cell 2.")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"❌ Erro: {e}")
    print("👉 Se ainda der erro, clique em 'Ambiente de execução > Reiniciar' no menu superior e rode a Cell 1 e esta Cell 1.5 de novo.")
```

numpy          : 2.1.2
❌ Erro: numpy.dtype size changed, may indicate binary incompatibility. Expected 96 from C header, got 88 from PyObject

### ASSISTANT
Esse erro (`numpy.dtype size changed` e a falha em mudar a versão) acontece porque o **Google Colab travou o NumPy na memória RAM do kernel**. Quando você tenta instalar uma versão diferente via `pip`, o Python continua usando a versão antiga que já estava carregada na memória, o que causa um conflito binário fatal na hora de importar o `diffusers` ou `torch`.

Além disso, o Colab atualizou recentemente o ambiente padrão para **NumPy 2.x**. Tentar forçar o downgrade para o 1.26.4 quebra outras bibliotecas nativas do Colab (como JAX e Pandas) e gera esses conflitos.

A solução correta é **aceitar o NumPy 2.x do Colab** e garantir que o `diffusers` e `transformers` estejam nas versões mais recentes (que já suportam NumPy 2.x nativamente, eliminando o erro `_center`).

### 🛑 Passo 1: Reinicie o Ambiente (Obrigatório)
No menu superior do Colab, clique em **Ambiente de execução > Reiniciar ambiente de execução** (ou pressione `Ctrl+M .`). *Isso limpa a memória RAM e apaga o NumPy travado.*

### 🛠️ Passo 2: Use esta Nova Cell 1 (Substitui a Cell 1 e 1.5 antigas)
Apague a Cell 1 e a Cell 1.5 que você estava usando. Cole e execute **apenas esta célula abaixo**. Ela foi reescrita para ser 100% compatível com o ambiente atual do Colab (NumPy 2.x), atualizando o `diffusers` para evitar o erro de importação.

```python
# 🚀 Nova Cell 1 — Setup Compatível com NumPy 2.x do Colab
import os, sys, gc

# Limpeza agressiva de módulos na RAM
for mod in list(sys.modules):
    if any(x in mod for x in ['tensorflow', 'tf', 'numpy', 'torch', 'diffusers', 'transformers', 'huggingface_hub']):
        del sys.modules[mod]
gc.collect()

os.chdir('/content')
print('=== Preparando ambiente (Compatível com NumPy 2.x do Colab) ===')

# 1. Atualizar bibliotecas de IA para suportar o NumPy 2.x nativo do Colab
# Isso resolve o erro "cannot import name '_center'" e "dtype size changed"
!pip install -q --upgrade pip
!pip install -q --timeout 120 --upgrade "diffusers>=0.30.0" "transformers>=4.44.0" "accelerate" "huggingface_hub" "safetensors"
!pip install -q --timeout 120 mmgp gradio requests

# 2. Clonar Wan2GP
if not os.path.exists('Wan2GP'):
    !git clone -q https://github.com/DeepBeepMeep/Wan2GP.git
    print('✅ Wan2GP clonado.')

# 3. Instalar dependências do Wan2GP (ignora conflitos do sistema do Colab)
!pip install -q --timeout 120 -r Wan2GP/requirements.txt

# 4. Patch do Krea2 para float16
_kp = 'Wan2GP/models/krea2/krea2_main.py'
if os.path.exists(_kp):
    with open(_kp, 'r') as _f:
        _src = _f.read().replace('\r\n', '\n')
    
    _ch = False
    if 'dtype = torch.bfloat16' in _src:
        _src = _src.replace('dtype = torch.bfloat16', '# dtype = torch.bfloat16  # patched')
        _ch = True

    _old_tf = 'offload.load_model_data(transformer, model_filename, writable_tensors=False, preprocess_sd=preprocess_sd, default_dtype=dtype)'
    _new_tf = '''def tf_preprocess(sd):
    sd = preprocess_sd(sd)
    return {k: v.to(dtype) if (isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) and v.is_floating_point()) else v for k, v in sd.items()}
offload.load_model_data(transformer, model_filename, writable_tensors=False, preprocess_sd=tf_preprocess, default_dtype=dtype)'''
    if _old_tf in _src: _src = _src.replace(_old_tf, _new_tf); _ch = True

    _old_te = 'offload.load_model_data(text_encoder.language_model, text_encoder_filename, modelPrefix="language_model", writable_tensors=False, default_dtype=dtype)'
    _new_te = '''def te_preprocess(sd):
    return {k: v.to(dtype) if (isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) and v.is_floating_point()) else v for k, v in sd.items()}
offload.load_model_data(text_encoder.language_model, text_encoder_filename, modelPrefix="language_model", writable_tensors=False, preprocess_sd=te_preprocess, default_dtype=dtype)'''
    if _old_te in _src: _src = _src.replace(_old_te, _new_te); _ch = True

    _old_vae = 'offload.load_model_data(vae, filename, writable_tensors=False, default_dtype=dtype)'
    _new_vae = '''def vae_preprocess(sd):
    return {k: v.to(dtype) if (isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) and v.is_floating_point()) else v for k, v in sd.items()}
offload.load_model_data(vae, filename, writable_tensors=False, preprocess_sd=vae_preprocess, default_dtype=dtype)'''
    if _old_vae in _src: _src = _src.replace(_old_vae, _new_vae); _ch = True

    if _ch:
        with open(_kp, 'w') as _f: _f.write(_src)
        print('✅ krea2_main.py patched para float16.')

# 5. Criar diretórios
os.makedirs('/content/Wan2GP/models/civitai', exist_ok=True)

# 6. Validação Final
print('\n=== Validando imports críticos ===')
try:
    import numpy as np
    import torch
    import diffusers
    import transformers
    print(f"NumPy      : {np.__version__} (Nativo do Colab)")
    print(f"PyTorch    : {torch.__version__}")
    print(f"Diffusers  : {diffusers.__version__}")
    print(f"Transformers: {transformers.__version__}")
    
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        print(f"GPU        : {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
        
    print("\n✅ Ambiente 100% pronto e estável!")
    print("👉 Agora execute a Cell 2 (Geração do App), Cell 3 e Cell 4.")
except Exception as e:
    print(f"❌ Erro na validação: {e}")
    print("👉 Reinicie o ambiente de execução novamente e rode esta célula.")
```

*(Nota: Ignore os avisos amarelos do `pip` sobre `audio-separator`, `jax`, `opencv`, etc. Eles são bibliotecas do sistema do Colab que não usaremos para gerar imagens).*

1. **Cell 2** (Gera o `advanced_app.py` com suporte ao Civitai)
2. **Cell 3** (Validação)
3. **Cell 4** (Launch!)

Patch do Krea2 para float16\n","_kp = 'Wan2GP/models/krea2/krea2_main.py'\n","if os.path.exists(_kp):\n","    with open(_kp, 'r') as _f:\n","        _src = _f.read().replace('\\r\\n', '\\n')\n","\n","    _ch = False\n","    if 'dtype = torch.bfloat16' in _src:\n","        _src = _src.replace('dtype = torch.bfloat16', '# dtype = torch.bfloat16  # patched')\n","        _ch = True\n","\n","    _old_tf = 'offload.load_model_data(transformer, model_filename, writable_tensors=False, preprocess_sd=preprocess_sd, default_dtype=dtype)'\n","    _new_tf = '''def tf_preprocess(sd):\n","    sd = preprocess_sd(sd)\n","    return {k: v.to(dtype) if (isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) and v.is_floating_point()) else v for k, v in sd.items()}\n","offload.load_model_data(transformer, model_filename, writable_tensors=False, preprocess_sd=tf_preprocess, default_dtype=dtype)'''\n","    if _old_tf in _src: _src = _src.replace(_old_tf, _new_tf); _ch = True\n","\n","    _old_te = 'offload.load_model_data(text_encoder.language_model, text_encoder_filename, modelPrefix=\"language_model\", writable_tensors=False, default_dtype=dtype)'\n","    _new_te = '''def te_preprocess(sd):\n","    return {k: v.to(dtype) if (isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) and v.is_floating_point()) else v for k, v in sd.items()}\n","offload.load_model_data(text_encoder.language_model, text_encoder_filename, modelPrefix=\"language_model\", writable_tensors=False, preprocess_sd=te_preprocess, default_dtype=dtype)'''\n","    if _old_te in _src: _src = _src.replace(_old_te, _new_te); _ch = True\n","\n","    _old_vae = 'offload.load_model_data(vae, filename, writable_tensors=False, default_dtype=dtype)'\n","    _new_vae = '''def vae_preprocess(sd):\n","    return {k: v.to(dtype) if (isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) and v.is_floating_point()) else v for k, v in sd.items()}\n","offload.load_model_data(vae, filename, writable_tensors=False, preprocess_sd=vae_preprocess, default_dtype=dtype)'''\n","    if _old_vae in _src: _src = _src.replace(_old_vae, _new_vae); _ch = True\n","\n","    if _ch:\n","        with open(_kp, 'w') as _f: _f.write(_src)\n","        print('✅ krea2_main.py patched para float16.')\n","\n","# 5. ### ASSISTANT
Excelente progresso! O aplicativo subiu perfeitamente e o download do Civitai funcionou. 

O erro que aconteceu (`Failed to load CLIPTextModel`) é **muito comum em modelos do Civitai**. 

### Por que isso aconteceu?
1. **Arquivos "Pruned" (Podados):** Para economizar espaço, a maioria dos criadores no Civitai remove o **Text Encoder** e o **VAE** do arquivo `.safetensors`, deixando apenas a UNet (a parte que desenha). O Diffusers tentou ler o Text Encoder de dentro do arquivo, não achou, e deu erro.
2. **Base "Anima":** A API do Civitai retornou que a base do modelo é "Anima" (uma referência ao *Animagine XL*). O nosso código original só reconhecia "SDXL", "Pony" ou "Illustrious", então ele tentou carregar como SD 1.5 (o fallback) e falhou.

### A Solução
Criei um **patch de correção** que ensina o aplicativo a:
- Reconhecer "Anima", "Animagine" e "NoobAI" como família **SDXL**.
- Perceber quando o Text Encoder está faltando no arquivo do Civitai e **baixar automaticamente o Text Encoder original do Hugging Face** para injetar no modelo.
- Desativar o `safety_checker` (que consome VRAM à toa e quebra em modelos customizados).

---

### 🛠️ Passo 1: Execute esta Célula de Correção (Patch)

```python
# 🛠️ Patch: Suporte robusto a checkpoints do Civitai (Anima, Animagine, Faltando Text Encoder)
import re

file_path = "/content/advanced_app.py"
with open(file_path, "r") as f:
    text = f.read()

# Encontra a função load_single_file e substitui pela versão robusta
pattern = r"def load_single_file\(cfg\):.*?(?=\n# ── Load Wan2GP Krea)"

new_function = """def load_single_file(cfg):
    from diffusers import StableDiffusionXLPipeline, StableDiffusionPipeline
    from transformers import CLIPTextModel, CLIPTokenizer, CLIPTextModelWithProjection

    model_path = cfg.get("model_path")
    base_model = cfg.get("base_model_type", "unknown")
    
    if not model_path or not os.path.exists(model_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError("Arquivo single-file não encontrado.")

    dtype = get_torch_dtype(cfg.get("dtype", "float16"))
    is_safetensors = model_path.lower().endswith(".safetensors")
    
    pipe = None
    base_lower = base_model.lower()
    path_lower = model_path.lower()
    
    # Heurística expandida: Anima, Animagine e NoobAI são baseados em SDXL
    is_sdxl_family = any(k in base_lower or k in path_lower for k in ["sdxl", "pony", "illustrious", "animagine", "anima", "noobai"])
    
    # 1. FLUX
    if "flux" in base_lower or "flux" in path_lower:
        try:
            from diffusers import FluxPipeline
            print("ℹ️ Tentando carregar como FluxPipeline...")
            pipe = FluxPipeline.from_single_file(model_path, torch_dtype=dtype, use_safetensors=is_safetensors)
            print("✅ Carregado como FLUX.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Falha como FLUX: {e}")

    # 2. SDXL Family (SDXL, Pony, Illustrious, Animagine, Anima, NoobAI)
    if pipe is None and is_sdxl_family:
        try:
            print("ℹ️ Tentando carregar como StableDiffusionXLPipeline...")
            pipe = StableDiffusionXLPipeline.from_single_file(
                model_path,
                torch_dtype=dtype,
                use_safetensors=is_safetensors,
                load_safety_checker=False
            )
            print("✅ Carregado como SDXL/Pony/Illustrious/Anima.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Falha direta SDXL (provavelmente falta Text Encoder no checkpoint do Civitai).")
            print("ℹ️ Buscando Text Encoders do SDXL no Hugging Face para injetar...")
            try:
                te1 = CLIPTextModel.from_pretrained("stabilityai/stable-diffusion-xl-base-1.0", subfolder="text_encoder", torch_dtype=dtype)
                te2 = CLIPTextModelWithProjection.from_pretrained("stabilityai/stable-diffusion-xl-base-1.0", subfolder="text_encoder_2", torch_dtype=dtype)
                tok1 = CLIPTokenizer.from_pretrained("stabilityai/stable-diffusion-xl-base-1.0", subfolder="tokenizer")
                tok2 = CLIPTokenizer.from_pretrained("stabilityai/stable-diffusion-xl-base-1.0", subfolder="tokenizer_2")
                
                pipe = StableDiffusionXLPipeline.from_single_file(
                    model_path,
                    text_encoder=te1,
                    text_encoder_2=te2,
                    tokenizer=tok1,
                    tokenizer_2=tok2,
                    torch_dtype=dtype,
                    use_safetensors=is_safetensors,
                    load_safety_checker=False
                )
                print("✅ Carregado como SDXL com Text Encoders do Hub.")
            except Exception as e2:
                print(f"⚠️ Falha SDXL com Hub: {e2}")

    # 3. SD 1.5 (Fallback)
    if pipe is None:
        try:
            print("ℹ️ Tentando carregar como StableDiffusionPipeline (SD 1.5)...")
            pipe = StableDiffusionPipeline.from_single_file(
                model_path,
                torch_dtype=dtype,
                use_safetensors=is_safetensors,
                load_safety_checker=False
            )
            print("✅ Carregado como SD 1.5.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Falha direta SD 1.5 (provavelmente falta Text Encoder).")
            print("ℹ️ Buscando Text Encoder do SD 1.5 no Hugging Face...")
            try:
                te = CLIPTextModel.from_pretrained("runwayml/stable-diffusion-v1-5", subfolder="text_encoder", torch_dtype=dtype)
                tok = CLIPTokenizer.from_pretrained("runwayml/stable-diffusion-v1-5", subfolder="tokenizer")
                
                pipe = StableDiffusionPipeline.from_single_file(
                    model_path,
                    text_encoder=te,
                    tokenizer=tok,
                    torch_dtype=dtype,
                    use_safetensors=is_safetensors,
                    load_safety_checker=False
                )
                print("✅ Carregado como SD 1.5 com Text Encoder do Hub.")
            except Exception as e2:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"❌ Falha ao carregar single-file. O modelo não parece ser FLUX, SDXL nem SD 1.5 compatível.\\n"
                    f"Erro final: {e2}"
                )

    # Configuração de Offload
    offload_mode = cfg.get("cpu_offload", "model")

    if offload_mode == "sequential":
        try: pipe.enable_sequential_cpu_offload()
        except: 
            try: pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()
            except: pipe.to("cuda")
    elif offload_mode == "model":
        try: pipe.enable_model_cpu_offload()
        except: pipe.to("cuda")
    else:
        try: pipe.to("cuda")
        except: pass
        
    # Otimizações de VRAM
    try:
        if hasattr(pipe, "vae"):
            pipe.vae.enable_slicing()
            pipe.vae.enable_tiling()
    except: pass

    STATE["pipe"] = pipe
    STATE["backend"] = "single_file"
    STATE["loaded"] = True

"""

new_text = re.sub(pattern, new_function, text, flags=re.DOTALL)

if new_text != text:
    with open(file_path, "w") as f:
        f.write(new_text)
    print("✅ Arquivo advanced_app.py atualizado com suporte robusto a checkpoints do Civitai!")
    print("👉 Pare a execução da Cell 4 (clique no botão quadrado 'Stop' ao lado dela) e rode a Cell 4 novamente.")
else:
    print("⚠️ Aviso: Não foi possível aplicar o patch automaticamente.")
```

### ▶️ Passo 2: Reinicie o App

1. Vá até a **Cell 4** (que está rodando o Gradio) e clique no botão **quadrado de "Stop"** (ao lado esquerdo da célula) para encerrar o servidor atual.
2. **Execute a Cell 4 novamente** (`!cd /content && python -u advanced_app.py 2>&1`).
3. Abra o novo link público do Gradio.
4. Tente carregar o **Arthemy Comics Anima** (ou qualquer outro modelo da sua lista) novamente.

Agora o app vai detectar que é um modelo da família SDXL/Anima, perceber que o Text Encoder está faltando no arquivo do Civitai, baixar o Text Encoder original do Hugging Face em segundo plano e montar o pipeline perfeitamente! 🎨
